    190: function uepDiagSafe(value,depth=0){
    191:   if(depth>6)return '[DEPTH_LIMIT]';
    192:   if(value==null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return value;
    193:   if(typeof value==='string')return value.replace(/[A-Z0-9._%+-]+@[A-Z0-9.-]+\.[A-Z]{2,}/gi,'[EMAIL_REDACTED]').slice(0,4000);
    194:   if(Array.isArray(value))return value.slice(0,30).map(v=>uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1));
    195:   if(typeof value==='object'){
    196:     const out={};
    197:     for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(value)){
    198:       if(/token|secret|password|authorization|cookie|credential|private|email|name/i.test(k)){out[k]='[REDACTED]';continue;}
    199:       out[k]=uepDiagSafe(v,depth+1);
    200:     }

    701: async function batchUpdateSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, data, attempt=0) {
    702:   const items=(Array.isArray(data)?data:[]).filter(x=>x&&x.range&&Array.isArray(x.values));
    703:   if(!items.length)return {totalUpdatedRows:0};
    704:   const url=new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values:batchUpdate`);
    705:   const response=await fetch(url,{
    706:     method:"POST",
    711:     let detail="";try{detail=(await response.json())?.error?.message||"";}catch{}
    712:     if((response.status===429||response.status>=500)&&attempt<4){
    713:       const retryAfter=Number(response.headers.get("retry-after")||0);
    714:       const waitMs=retryAfter>0?retryAfter*1000:1000*Math.pow(2,attempt);
    715:       await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,waitMs));
    716:       return batchUpdateSheetValues(token,spreadsheetId,items,attempt+1);
    717:     }
    718:     throw new Error(`구글시트 일괄 저장 실패 (${response.status})${detail?`: ${detail}`:""}`);
    719:   }
    720:   return response.json();
